# Gielinor Go species

All 1297 species in `species.json`, with the wiki page, the infobox picture, and a biome.

Biomes were assigned from each wiki page's Location table (place names and map coordinates), then checked by
hand. The **primary** biome is the most typical home. The **secondary** is the next most common, or `-`.
A species marked quest-only never turns wild. Treat the biomes as a best-effort guide, not canon.

## Biome counts (primary)

| Biome | Species |
| --- | --- |
| Cave & Dungeon | 229 |
| Crypt & Haunted | 185 |
| Town & Castle | 175 |
| Otherworldly | 139 |
| Snow & Mountain | 101 |
| Coast & Sea | 88 |
| Swamp & Jungle | 82 |
| Desert | 80 |
| Forest & Woodland | 80 |
| Wilderness | 62 |
| Grassland & Farmland | 41 |
| Volcano & Fire | 35 |
